# xLearn v2 — status

Cross-sprint living tracker for the v2 build. Updated per the
[status protocol](sprints/README.md#status-protocol-way-of-working) whenever a task or sprint changes state (it's
baked into every `prompt-<id>.md`). Per-task detail lives in each [`sprints/sprint-<id>.md`](sprints/); the static
plan is [`build-plan.md`](build-plan.md); the source is the [rollout plan](rollout-plan.md); the one-page run order
with workstream diagrams is [`execution-order.md`](execution-order.md). Newest decisions at the
top of the log. v1's tracker is [`../v1/status.md`](../v1/status.md).

- **Build line:** v2 · **Phase:** **week 1** (the four spikes ran first under D41, 2026-09-25/26; S6 → D42), after the build-plan scaffold of 2026-09-24 (89 sprints: 86 plan +
  prompt pairs, 3 M6c outline cards; the ADR sign-off BP2; the calendar BP4). Done 2026-09-25: MI-0 (the H0 reboot),
  [spk-01](sprints/sprint-spk-01.md), [spk-02](sprints/sprint-spk-02.md) (MI-10 ✅) and [spk-03](sprints/sprint-spk-03.md)
  (WIF GO); 2026-09-26: [spk-04](sprints/sprint-spk-04.md) (S6: both fail under D28 → owner chose GPT-Live-1 with the
  fixed design, D42; [t6 §16](research/t6-realtime-interviewer.md#16-s6-results-spk-04-2026-09-26)). **Week 1:** [mi-01](sprints/sprint-mi-01.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (MI-2 infra#32, MI-3 infra#33, no pod rolled; `host-verify --cluster` 46/0/0); [mi-02](sprints/sprint-mi-02.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (MI-8: infra#36 + infra#37; `host-verify --cluster` 63/1/0, the WARN is TR-STEAL); [mi-07](sprints/sprint-mi-07.md) ✅ 2026-10-01 (MI-9: private `xlearn-evalpack` + probe, `v0.1.0` scaffold, helper infra#38, pull secrets + ImageRepository infra#51; re-run r-41); [mi-04](sprints/sprint-mi-04.md) 🔄 2026-09-28 (MI-5b: consoles on `ops.sujaykumar.dev`, infra#34 + infra#35, 4a ✅; ⛔ the owner's signed-in probe 4b); [m1-01](sprints/sprint-m1-01.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (PR #79, merge only: course manifest + golden, **item schema v1 frozen → `ev-schema-freeze`**, compose PG 18 / NATS 2.14); [m1-09](sprints/sprint-m1-09.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (PR #83, merge only: DSA in `curriculum/courses/dsa/`, glob loader + id/slug guards, curriculum `00002`/`00003` expand, CI `content` job, Example-1s rewritten → `ev-m1-statements` ✅); [mi-14](sprints/sprint-mi-14.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (MI-4: `sandbox-guards` + the VAP at `[Deny]`, infra#39–#41; Deny phase 24/0, `host-verify --cluster` 64/1/0); [mi-03](sprints/sprint-mi-03.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (MI-5 infra#42 + the review restart infra#43, MI-5a infra#44, all live; `host-verify --cluster` 64/1/0 with 12/12 policies; the fresh-pod JWKS proof joins the pending owner login smoke (owner, ask-18 option a)); [mi-05](sprints/sprint-mi-05.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (MI-6 / NATS N0, PR #85, merge only: dark in `v1.6.0`; `make nats-acl-test` green on NATS 2.14); [m3-01](sprints/sprint-m3-01.md) ✅ 2026-09-28 (T25/T26, PR #87, merge only: canon hashes + `contract_hash`, packlint, the pre-push fingerprint hook, curriculum `00004`; the owner's `ev-hook-install` next); next [ds-m1-01](sprints/sprint-ds-m1-01.md),
  [ds-m2-01](sprints/sprint-ds-m2-01.md), [ds-l-01](sprints/sprint-ds-l-01.md).
- **Live release:** **v1.5.2** (all seven services; signup closed in production since 2026-09-24, `SIGNUP_MODE=closed`).
- **Deploy mode:** release-semver tags. The v2 build ships as **1.x minors** until `v2.0.0` (D32, ADR-0034):
  `.release-line = 1`; every `xlearn-*` ImagePolicy is `>=1.0.0 <2.0.0`. Runner and evalpack are separate streams.
- **Audience:** owner only (D35). Testers exercise the non-owner paths; real learners arrive at v3.
- **Sprint merge rule (D40, 2026-09-25):** launching a sprint prompt is the owner's approval for every change it
  makes; every prompt ends with land-and-sync, and nothing waits on the owner mid-session ([decisions log](#decisions-log)).
- **Build tooling (D44, 2026-09-26):** the v2 build runs under a manager session that reports to
  [AI Builder](https://ai-builder.skriptvalley.com) ([ADR-0036](../adr/0036-ai-builder-execution-manager.md),
  [charter](manager/MANAGER.md)). The repo and this file stay canonical; AI Builder is the live layer. Dispatched
  sessions also report through `aib` (see [sprints/README.md](sprints/README.md#status-protocol-way-of-working),
  status protocol).

Earlier: **mi-02 ✅, MI-8**: `host-verify --cluster` now runs every ADR-0035 §3 check, read-only, with no alerting (infra#36 `d863abe`; infra#37 `356c936` lets it ride out a Flux revision roll). Live it gives 63 pass / 1 warn / 0 fail. The memory sum is +1.85 GiB inside the rule, and −1.15 GiB (a FAIL) with the runner counted, so MI-11a still gates MI-12. **TR-STEAL is firing** on the 7-day window, driven by the Longhorn fault loop; steal is back to 5–7% since the recovery ([capacity reads](#capacity-reads-tr-)). The `/root` copy is refreshed, the MI-11a `top` sampler runs until about 10-05, and S0 steal p95 is 8%. Earlier: **mi-01 ✅**: attempt 2 closed task 5. The owner recovered airlift's faulted Longhorn volume (ask-10, option a; 06:13–06:14Z) and forced the stalled `airlift` HelmRelease to reconcile. At 06:17Z, `host-verify --cluster` gave 46/0/0 and all 15 HelmReleases were Ready, the 11 chart ones on `project@0.3.0`. Earlier: mi-01: **MI-2 ✅** prune guards live (infra#32); **MI-3 ✅** chart `project` 0.3.0 with the §2.1 knob union, default-off, proven byte-identical by the new `hack/chart-diff.sh` (infra#33); no pod rolled. Earlier, 2026-09-26: spk-04 (S6) done: both shells failed a hard gate under D28; **D42**: the owner chose GPT-Live-1 with the fixed design, confirmed by a ≤ 1 h `ev-s6-recheck` that now gates ds-m6a-01's ADR-0032 acceptance; the M6b rows wait on it. One coach Deployment; Chrome/Edge + Safari. Earlier: spk-03 done: **WIF GO**, with `check_jti=false` on the one-rule issuer. The rule scope is `workspace:developer`, because the Console offers no `workspace:inference`, so ADR-0031 amendments are proposed for mi-12 ([t5 §15](research/t5-platform-ai.md#15-wif-spike-result-spk-03-2026-09-25)). Earlier: spk-02 done: **MI-10 ✅**, Spike P0–P3 GO and image volume GO. Block 3 narrowed the AppArmor remount rules; block 2 gave Q-C GO, the amd64 allowlists and the x86_64-only proposal; block 1 gave image volume GO and the GOCACHE seed. Earlier: spk-02 partial results, ⛔ rows open; earlier the same day, D40: the sprint merge directive applied to the plan, status and indexes; before
  that, 2026-09-24: the v2 build-plan session with the build plan, status, sprint + prompt scaffolds and ADR sign-off).

### NATS auth (N0–N4)

[ADR-0035 §2](../adr/0035-v2-operations-nats-auth-limits-capacity.md#2-nats-auth-nkey-users-fine-acls-server-first).
**Current stage: `n3`** (since **2026-09-29T14:26:46Z**; `PIN_NATS_STAGE=n3` in `infra/hack/host-verify.sh` and the node's `/root` copy). Every connection is on its own nkey and `legacy` is deny `>`. The N3 timestamp gates [l-01](sprints/sprint-l-01.md) (≥ 24 h, so from 2026-09-30T14:27Z) and M3. Public keys: practice `UCZONB…`, review `UDECHS…`, assessment `UCJVF5…`, identity `UALKQK…`, ops `UAEO5F…` (full keys in `infra/infrastructure/messaging/release.yaml`).

### M3 hard entry checklist (live)

Copied verbatim from [rollout §5](rollout-plan.md#5-m3-hard-entry-checklist); ticked here as items land. A red item
blocks the M3 UI sprint ([m3-11](sprints/sprint-m3-11.md)).

- [x] Spike P0–P3 **GO** and the image-volume spike **GO** (MI-10). ✅ 2026-09-25: spk-01 + spk-02 ([t3 §16.4](research/t3-sandbox.md#164-mi-10-verdict-and-proposed-adr-0030-deltas))
- [ ] MI-4, MI-5, **MI-5a**, **MI-7 (N3)**, MI-9, MI-11, **MI-11a**, MI-12 and MI-13 done (MI-4 ✅ 2026-09-28; MI-5 ✅ and MI-5a ✅ 2026-09-28; MI-7 N3 ✅ 2026-09-29T14:26:46Z, the ≥ 24 h re-check is l-01's; MI-9 ✅ 2026-10-01, its ImagePolicy lands with MI-13 in m3-07; MI-11a ✅ 2026-10-01)
- [ ] **MI-8: `host-verify --cluster` (extended) green.** The memory sum, *with the runner's 3 GiB counted*, is ≤ capacity − 0.5 GiB; no OOMKills; PVCs < 60%; NATS `auth_required`; the NetworkPolicies are present. Green on 2026-10-01 after MI-11a (mi-08): 65/1/0, +1,323 Mi with the runner; re-read at M3 entry
- [x] T25/T26 tooling (pre-push fingerprint hook, packlint, `contract_hash`). ✅ 2026-09-28: m3-01, PR #87
- [ ] **14 pilot packs stamped** (Go, C++ and Python references; about 28–41 owner hours)
- [ ] `account.role` live (M1a), so the owner and tester cohort gates judge features
- [ ] TR-STEAL not firing (sar p95 read by `host-verify`), or R2 planned. As of 2026-09-28 it is firing on the 7-day window, driven by the Longhorn incident; see [capacity reads](#capacity-reads-tr-)
- [x] AB07–AB12 frozen. ✅ 2026-09-28: ds-m3-01, PR #89 + ds-m3-02, PR #94
- [ ] The last Hostinger weekly image is ≤ 7 days old

*Key:* MI-4 = mi-14 · MI-5/5a = mi-03 · MI-7 (N3) = mi-06 (+ l-01 re-check) · MI-8 = mi-02 · MI-9 = mi-07 (+ m3-07) ·
MI-10 = spk-01 + spk-02 · MI-11 = mi-09 + the host window · MI-11a = mi-08 · MI-12 = mi-10 · MI-13 = m3-07 ·
T25/T26 = m3-01 · 14 packs = `ev-packs-14` · `account.role` = m1-02 · AB07–AB12 = ds-m3-01 + ds-m3-02 merged (the
merge is the freeze, D40).

## Flag inventory

[ADR-0034 §2](../adr/0034-v2-release-labelling-gating-and-rollback.md#2-feature-gating-three-tiers-no-flag-service):
T-1 build-time · T-2 runtime env (infra PR) · T-3 cohort (`account.role`, never the JWT). **≤ 6 live non-kill
flags**, each with an owning and a removal milestone. Kill switches and operating modes are permanent and listed
separately. Rows marked *planned* are added live by the owning sprint.

| judge cohort gate (`judgeCohortOnly`) | T-3 cohort (code default) | gateway (judge BFF) | M3 ([m3-09](sprints/sprint-m3-09.md)) | GA ([ga-01](sprints/sprint-ga-01.md)) | planned |
| platform-AI cohort gate (`platformAICohortOnly`) | T-3 cohort (code default) | judge | M4 ([m4-02](sprints/sprint-m4-02.md)) | GA ([ga-01](sprints/sprint-ga-01.md)) | planned |
| `COURSE_STATUS_OVERRIDE` | T-2 | every `xlearn-*` | P ([p-02](sprints/sprint-p-02.md)) | GA ([ga-01](sprints/sprint-ga-01.md)): remove, or promote to an operating mode (decided in ga-01's session, D40) | planned |
| interviewer cohort gate (`interviewAudience = "cohort"`) | T-1 code default (T-3 audience) | coach (`/api/interviews/*`) | M6a ([m6a-01](sprints/sprint-m6a-01.md)) | v2.1.0 ([m6b-04](sprints/sprint-m6b-04.md)) | planned |

## Artboards

Static HTML under `design-system/screens/v2/` (preview-only). States: ⬜ not drafted → 🔄 drafting (the `ds-*`
session is running) → ✅ **frozen (PR #N, date)** → consumed by `<sprint>`. **The `ds-*` merge is the freeze
(D40):** the design sprint records it here itself, in its own PR or a follow-up docs PR merged the same way, and a
consuming UI sprint only verifies it (repairing a missing row if needed). The owner may review a board after it
merges; a change to a frozen board is a follow-up design PR
([status protocol](sprints/README.md#design-sprints-the-owner-review-gate)).
Register: [build-plan → Artboard register](build-plan.md#artboard-register).

## Owner calendar events

Owner-only actions are **calendar events, not sprints** (BP4, tentative and owner-booked). **Since D40 nothing
waits on the owner mid-session.** An owner-only action is done **before launch** of the sprint that needs it: that
prompt's `## Before you launch (owner)` block lists it, and launching attests it's done. A sprint may prepare one
(runbook, scripts). If a prerequisite turns out missing, the session lands what doesn't depend on it and marks the
gap ⛔ here. Events that only froze boards or awaited a sign-off are **automatic** (second table). Tick the state here
(✅ + date) when it happens.

(infra PR updates the pull secret in `xlearn` and `flux-system`). The GHCR quota row is read the same way (doc debt F6-26).

| Credential | Scope | Created | Expires | Rotate by | Last checked | State |
|------------|-------|---------|---------|-----------|--------------|-------|
| evalpack machine-user PAT (classic, `read:packages`; machine user `sujaykumar-bot`) | GHCR pull of `xlearn-evalpack` | 2026-09-28 (`ev-machine-user`) | **2027-09-28** | **2027-09-14** | 2026-10-01 ([mi-07](sprints/sprint-mi-07.md) re-run) | ✅ in the cluster since 2026-10-01 (infra#51): `xlearn-evalpack-pull` in `xlearn` and `flux-system`; the ImageRepository scans Ready with it. Rotation: infra README "Secrets" |
| GHCR private-package quota (`xlearn-evalpack`; an allowance, not a credential) | package storage + data transfer of pulls from outside GitHub Actions (the node's kubelet, Flux scans) | — | allowance ⬜ **owner: read Settings → Billing** (the agent's `gh` token has no `user`/`read:packages` scope, so the plan isn't readable) | prune: [m3-02](sprints/sprint-m3-02.md)'s `retention` job (keeps the newest 10 tagged versions, the policy pick and their platform manifests; dry-run unless dispatched with `prune: delete`) | 2026-10-01 (m3-02) | 🔄 `0.2.0` (format 1, 0 items, multi-arch): amd64 layers 205 B (build log); the retention dry-run listed 4 versions, 0 candidates. `0.1.0`: OCI manifest 476 B. The single layer holds one `manifest.json`, so the image is about 1 KB; its blob sizes need `read:packages`. Scans every 5 min. t1 §6.1 sizes the real pack at ≲ 400 MB stored, ≲ 200 MB/month |

The reboot ended the sampler and emptied the node's `/tmp`; the sampler wasn't restarted (`sar` covers the rest), so there is no 2026-09-27 collection. **Result (mi-02, 2026-09-28):** steal p50 3%, p95 8%, max 20%, worst 60-minute mean 7.0% over the 1,305 samples ([t3 §15](research/t3-sandbox.md#15-s0-results-read-only-production-facts-2026-09-24-owner-approved)). The x86_64-only pod profile is proposed. The AppArmor `remount,` finding is closed with `ro`-only scoped rules. Image volume GO with the kubelet defaults; the GOCACHE seed is measured | [t3 §16.2–16.4](research/t3-sandbox.md#162-p3-amd64-replay-spk-02) | ✅ 2026-09-25, both sessions: env A restored to its baseline. That means `k3s-uninstall.sh`; the registry, images, buildx refs, host files, sysctls and `/root/xl-spike` removed; the packages the spike added purged (the package list equals the pre-spike baseline); and apport and the kernel-meta holds as found. ## Capacity reads (TR-*)

Measured on demand; nothing alerts (D34). Read with `host-verify --cluster` (MI-8 extension, [mi-02](sprints/sprint-mi-02.md))
after host changes and before contract, erase or GA tags. Triggers and responses R0 → R3:
[ADR-0035 §5](../adr/0035-v2-operations-nats-auth-limits-capacity.md#5-capacity-the-memory-sum-rule-triggers-and-ordered-responses).

## Hand-offs

Cross-sprint hand-offs (values, env, file paths one sprint leaves for another). Also stated in the PR description.
**Folded into target plan?** separates *recorded* from *applied* (doc debt F5-16): ✅ + PR = the target's plan (and prompt)
carry the hand-off; ◐ = partly; ⬜ = recorded here only, so the target session reads this row (its dispatch envelope carries it)
or a doc-fold PR folds it first; — = nothing to fold (a build sprint's hand-off the target reads at launch, or a closed row).

| From | To | What | State | Folded into target plan? |
|------|----|------|-------|--------------------------|
| [mi-05](sprints/sprint-mi-05.md) | [mi-06](sprints/sprint-mi-06.md) | **The N1 block and its proof.** Paste the output of `make nats-acl-render NKEYS=<pubkeys.env> LEGACY=allow FORMAT=yaml` (public keys only: `<identity>=U…` per line for practice, review, assessment, identity, judge, coach and ops; the legacy password is random and never used) under the messaging HelmRelease's `spec.values.config` (chart key `config.merge`, checked against the pinned chart 2.14.6 with `helm template`). N3 re-renders with `LEGACY=deny`, N4 (mi-11) with `none`. Client env per service at N2: `NATS_NKEY_SEED_FILE=/var/run/secrets/nats/seed` (the prefix defaults to `_INBOX_<svc>`). Connection names are `xlearn-<svc>:<stream>:pub\|cons`, and `/connz?auth=true` shows an nkey user's public key in `authorized_user` (seen on 2.14 in the test). **identity's N2 PR adds `NATS_URL` and its seed together** (ADR-0035 §2); with `NATS_URL` set, a missing seed makes the pod exit (fail closed) | ✅ closed by mi-06 (2026-09-29): rendered at `v1.6.0`, `authorized_user` shows the nkey on 2.14.6; judge/coach dropped until m3-07/l-01 | — |

## Accepted-risk register

| Risk | Decision | Mitigation in v2 | Revisit |
|------|----------|------------------|---------|
| **No off-node backups**: a data-loss window up to ~7 days (the weekly Hostinger image); an R-d restore brings back erases done since the snapshot | **D12** ([ADR-0028](../adr/0028-object-storage-and-backups.md)) | MI-2 prune guards; a manual snapshot right before every contract, erase or GA tag; the weekly image ≤ 7 days old before risky steps; the R-d procedure re-runs listed erases; the privacy notice states the window | D12's triggers (backups to the second VPS); before the opening (v3) |
| **No alerting**: a failure is seen only when the owner looks | **D34** ([ADR-0035 §3](../adr/0035-v2-operations-nats-auth-limits-capacity.md#3-no-alerting-in-v2-owner-d34)) | `host-verify --cluster` (MI-8) after host changes and in tag checklists; landscape + kubescope; verify-by-looking after every tag; the PAT expiry date above | **before the first real invite** (a v3 opening gate): adopt the kept healthchecks.io design or re-accept in writing |
| Untrusted code runs on the production node | D21 ([ADR-0030](../adr/0030-runner-technology-and-host-hardening.md)) | R1 + H0 + D22 patch cadence; `sandbox-guards`; runner at the lowest priority; signup never `open` while the runner is on the node | any D21 trigger (open signup, unpatched reachable LPE > 7 d, TR-STEAL) → R2 |
| No staging environment | ADR-0034 §5 | compose at prod parity, `-rc` images, cohort dark launch on prod | a second node, or > 10 active learners after v3 with a contract migration touching their data |
| Single gateway replica (cache epoch + in-process limiter) | rollout §12 | recorded scale-out blocker | before any gateway scale-out |

## Decisions log

Notable calls not (yet) worth a full ADR, newest first. Promote to an ADR if they harden.

| Date | Decision | Notes |
|------|----------|-------|
| 2026-10-02 | **m3-15: the runner release as built (`runner-v1.0.0`).**<br>• **No C++ PCH.** GCC's PCH for `xl_prelude.hpp` is 187 MB, and three builds gave three hashes: it would break the reproducible digest, and runtime generation would put ~187 MB of page cache into `runner/`. It costs ~0.7 s more CPU per C++ compile.<br>• **Image size:** `linux/amd64` 236 MB compressed (gzip-9), arm64 224 MB. One layer.<br>• **BuildKit pin:** `moby/buildkit:v0.33.1@sha256:cec9f139f45e…`, the same in `runner-release.yml`, CI's two lanes and the Dockerfile's local recipe. `deploy/workflows_test.go` fails if they drift. Renovate (mi-11) bumps it with the `ARG` pins.<br>• **`deploy/runner.release-line` = `1`**, the runner's major guard (`runner-release.yml`'s `guard` job). It is documented in `docs/git-strategy.md` "Runner stream", the file's only record, since ADR-0034 §1.5 doesn't name it.<br>• **GOROOT gets a fixed past mtime (`GOROOT_MTIME`), not `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH`.** Found in the first image: the go command's module index (in GOCACHE) keys std packages on their files' mtimes, so a seed built before the mtime normalization failed every read-only compile ("package runtime is not in std"). The fixed mtime also keeps go@1.26's `profile_sha256` commit-independent. The seed keeps its future mtime.<br>• **`HOME=/dev/null` in the build's rootfs stage.** rc.1's local rebuild under Docker Desktop's Rosetta differed from CI by exactly `root/.cache/rosetta`. With the guard, the same rebuild reproduced rc.1 bit for bit, so content is unchanged. Hence rc.2.<br>• **`runner-release.yml` smokes every tag:** the pushed image by digest, dev mode, `make runner-acceptance SUBSET=prod`. That's task 8's smoke on amd64, which this Mac can't run natively. The GitHub release waits for it and reads each `profile_sha256` from the pushed image.<br>• **Package visibility:** `xlearn-runner` was **public at first push**, so the anonymous pull of `:1.0.0-rc.1`, `-rc.2` and `:1.0.0` works. No owner item.<br>• **VM rehearsal (task 6): not run, ⛔.** multipassd isn't running on the build Mac, and starting the system LaunchDaemon needs root. mi-09's sandbox block isn't on infra `main` yet either. So there was no `host-verify --expect-sandbox` result (the expected `sandbox.seccomp` FAIL wasn't observed) and no VM-only VAP widening.<br>• **Suite calls:**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– E probes all seven channels in one job per language; the denied ones die of SIGSYS. Only the writable ones get the job N / job N+1 pair (in practice `/w` for Go and Python, none for C++, whose exec filter has no `openat`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `make runner-acceptance` builds the test binary (`go test -c`) and runs it directly, so a `<(sops …)` token file reaches it.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `ONLY=` is a debugging aid; its report says it isn't a release gate.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– The image lanes' path filter is the plan's plus `internal/platform/harness/`, `go.mod`/`go.sum` and the two workflow files. | Run r-47; [PR #116](https://github.com/sujaykumarsuman/xlearn/pull/116), [PR #117](https://github.com/sujaykumarsuman/xlearn/pull/117). **CI in-image run** (37029026421): A–H + calibration green, 153 jobs, rotations 15/15, `runner/` drift +104 KiB after 1,000 case cgroups, `nr_dying` settled in 2 s, canary median 144,480 µs. **Seed tree hash (amd64)** `a9ac8a1c…`, identical under QEMU/Rosetta and native. |
| 2026-10-01 | **m3-04: the launch profiles as built.**<br>• **Provisional TL multipliers** (`calibrated: false`; mi-10 calibrates): go 1.0 (the baseline), **cpp 1.0**, **python 6.5**. Method: over the perf cases, the max of CPU(lang ref) / CPU(Go ref), Go CPU floored at 10 ms, rounded up to the next 0.5, floor 1.0, on CI amd64 `runner-it`. Ratios: cpp 0.41–0.77; python 2.50–6.32, the max being running-median, a heap per insert. Judge multiplies the pack's Go TL by the served value; the runner enforces only the TL it is sent.<br>• **Allowlist additions beyond t3 §16.2** (amd64; the arm64 dev lists match):<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– go exec `close`: the generated harness closes fd 4 after its frame (m3-02's bytes); without it every Go case is a SIGSYS.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– cpp exec `getpid gettid rt_sigaction rt_sigprocmask tgkill`: abort()'s calls. Without them a failed `assert` is a SIGSYS (counted, the runner rotates). With them the case, as its pid namespace's init, ignores its own SIGABRT and abort traps (SIGTRAP arm64, SIGSEGV amd64) → RE.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– cpp compile `dup2`: `-pipe` wires cc1plus \| as through posix_spawn file actions; ENOSYS failed every compile on amd64.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– python: none.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;Nothing comes from the dangerous set; splice/vmsplice/tee are KILL in every filter (no toolchain called them).<br>• **arm64 dev lists:** the amd64 names translated (`*at` forms, no `arch_prctl`). The delta was searched with the whole runner-it lane KILL-default on an arm64 Docker VM, every kill read from the kernel audit log: none. m3-03's LOG switch is retired.<br>• **Python lint location:** a Go tokenizer in `runnerapi/lint`, run by judge before enqueue (REJECTED without a compile). `__future__` is allowed beside the t3 list; Go's `unicode` rides with `unicode/*`.<br>• **Deviations from the plan:**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– Go: `-vet=off` dropped (not a `go build` flag). `HOME` unset, `GOPATH=/w/gopath` (m3-03's hand-off: with `HOME` set, `GOTELEMETRY=off` still writes `$HOME/.config/go/telemetry`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– Python exec: `python3 -s -P -S -B`, not `-I`, because `-I` implies `-E`, which drops `PYTHONHASHSEED=0`; the env is the profile's own. The compile step checks `XL_REQUIRES` from the AST, so a missing method is a CE in `__main__.py`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– C++: node structs live in the generated `zz_xl_harness.cpp`, only those the signature uses, so `xl_prelude.hpp` stays static and PCH-able.<br>• **No m3-02-vs-plan byte difference:** m3-02's README is the `@1` spec unchanged, so there is no `@2` candidate; a compatibility golden pins m3-02's fixture fd-3/fd-4 bytes.<br>• **PCH and `.pyc`:** handed to m3-15. A C++ compile is ~1 s CPU on CI amd64 without a PCH. Python reads Debian's stdlib `__pycache__`; `-B` never writes. | Run r-46, [PR #115](https://github.com/sujaykumarsuman/xlearn/pull/115). Memory baselines (CI amd64): go 2,560 KiB, cpp 256 KiB, python 3,324 KiB. The Go compile with the seed takes 204 ms CPU in the jail; the seed builds in 20.7 s. |
| 2026-10-01 | **m1-07: D27 assist capture, the coach mode gate and L18 — the calls `v1.7.0` ships with.**<br>• **Fail closed, twice.** If a chat's state lookup can't answer — practice `/attempts/open`, review's due queue or assessment `/mocks/live` — it returns **503 `coach_state_unavailable`** and nothing is sent. v1 fell back to `attempt` and forwarded. If a confirmed D27 chat's assist record fails, it returns **503 `assist_unavailable`**, and again nothing is sent. m1-06's "treat as live" is still right for withholding, but the D27 check needs the attempt id, so the chat can't guess it. The thread read omits `gate` instead (it is display only).<br>• **The daily cap is the UTC day** (one clock, no identity lookup): 300 messages per account per UTC date, counted in `coach.message_quota_day`. **There is no F8 copy delta to flag.** The frozen AB01 F8c already says "It resets at 00:00 UTC (&lt;local time&gt; your time)" (ds-m1-01 decision 2), so the SPA shows the reset as a local clock time computed from `Retry-After`. A day per account timezone stays a follow-up: the gateway would have to pass the account's timezone.<br>• **We accept the residual cap-on-429 race. This is this sprint's decision, not t5 §9's.** Before recording a D27 assist, the gateway runs coach's read-only `GET /admission` probe, so a chat that L18 would refuse never caps the attempt. The probe is not a reservation, though: a concurrent send can take the last slot between the probe and the chat. That chat then gets a 429 after the assist was recorded. This can happen at most once per attempt, because the record is idempotent and later chats skip the confirm. A provider failure after the record still caps the attempt (t5 §9: the learner chose to ask).<br>• **MI-5b at tag time: still 🔄** ([mi-04](sprints/sprint-mi-04.md); the owner's signed-in probe 4b is pending). It is a soft gate (ADR-0033 §11). `v1.7.0` ships m1-06's Markdown renderer without it, as the plan allows.<br>• **M1c readiness (hand-off to [m1-08](sprints/sprint-m1-08.md)): ready.**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `hack/lint-dropped-columns.sh` is clean, with no allowlist left, and `internal/coach/contract_test.go` passes.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– A manual grep of `internal/*/store/gen/*.go`, the raw SQL in `internal/*/store/*.go` and the curriculum loader finds drop-list column names only in comments, JSON API field names and the `mock_completed` payload's `total_35`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– **No v1 conflict target is left, so none was switched here.** The weak-area upsert targets `(account_id, path_slug, week_of)` (m1-03); the concept upsert targets `(path_slug, slug)` (m1-09); `problem_section` is already a plain INSERT after the per-item delete.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `mock_session_scored_total_check` is present. Category and dimension validation exist, with 422 tests. No migration since `v1.6.0` carries `-- xlearn:contract`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– So m1-08 may drop all three v1 uniques and the drop-list columns.<br>• **Erase hand-off: l-01 coach erase must also delete `coach.message_quota_day` and `coach.key_default` (verify against the schema).** Both are per-user rows keyed by `account_id`. [l-01](sprints/sprint-l-01.md)'s coach list names only `api_key_config`, `coach_thread` and `outbox`. Noted in `data-model.md`.<br>• **The unowned `review`-mode slots have no owning sprint yet and stay empty:** the final submission (≤ 16 KiB) and the xLearn AI feedback from t5 §9's table. Proposed owner: [m4-03](sprints/sprint-m4-03.md), beside the pointer notes. In `v1.7.0`, only the pattern reaches a `review` prompt.<br>• **Smaller calls inside the plan:**<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `general` mode's `live_items` uses m1-06's `live()`: open attempts **and** due touches, at most 20, with curriculum titles when available. So the guard and withholding agree.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– On every non-item page, the gateway drops a client-sent `pattern`, `concepts` or `solution_facts`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– `/state`'s `coachAssistAt` reports the **open** attempt only. Once the attempt concludes, its recorded grade carries the cap, and the HUD chip doesn't linger on a solved problem; the compose walk caught the lingering chip.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;– Coach checks run in this order: `locked` first (defensive; the gateway already answers it), then the key, then L18 (streams, then per minute, then per day). | 2026-10-01 | **m3-02: the evalpack pipeline as built (format 1).**<br>• **Public code, private data:** every tool (packlint, `internal/packspec`, the registry, the harness, the checkers, the executor driver) is public Go; only pack contents are private.<br>• **One definition, one path:** `internal/platform/harness` (the Go half here, m3-04 extends), `internal/platform/checker` (m3-06 imports), `internal/packspec/gen` with t4 §4.1's six generators (m3-06 imports; `graph@1` is the edge-list generator). Leaf-import guard tests on harness and checker.<br>• **`Generate` returns `[]runnerapi.File`:** the plan's package-local `File` assumed `runnerapi` did not exist; m3-03 shipped it, so m3-04 has nothing to adapt.<br>• **The `/manifest.json` shape is exactly t1 §3.3's:** `{format_major, version, validated_against, items{id: {item_hash, accepts_contract_hashes, grader_kinds, files}}}`, hashes `sha256:<hex>`, `item_hash = sha256("xlearn.item@1\n" + sorted "path hash\n")`. Judge may decode it strictly.<br>• **Cases:** the id is `sha256("xlearn.case@1\n" + canonical input)`, over the input only. A class-mode line uses the public sample shape (`ops` with the constructor first, `args[0]` its arguments), not a separate `ctor` field. Seeds: the first 8 bytes of `sha256(item‖0‖cmd‖0‖argsJSON‖0‖index)`; a spec's canonical JSON stands in for `cmd`. `cases.jsonl.zst` is one zstd frame (single thread, default level, CRC, zero frames on).<br>• **The `fixture` course, not DSA items:** this keeps the public graded half of real items the owner's (`ev-packs-14`), decouples public CI from their contract edits and avoids fingerprint collisions. The `FIXTURE_CONTENT_DIR` overlay contract is handed to m3-05.<br>• **Executor:** one `docker run --network none --read-only --cpus 1 --memory 1g --pids-limit 128 --user 65534 --cap-drop ALL` per program. Images are pinned by index digest: `golang:1.26.8-bookworm`; `gcc:14` and `python:3.13-slim` for syntax only. A public stdlib-only driver is cross-compiled for the docker host. **The std-cache seed** is one read-only volume per Go image digest (`packlint exec --warm-cache`, `seed@1`, about 40 MB), copied into the tmpfs at start. CI restores it with `actions/cache`.<br>• **Provisional TL scale 1.0** on the pinned image. The wall kill is 1.5·TL + 0.5 s, and TLE = killed or CPU > TL. The memory baseline is a no-op Go program's peak RSS in the same container.<br>• **Multi-arch:** the pack image is `linux/amd64` + `linux/arm64` (data only, identical layers), so Apple-silicon compose can pull it.<br>• **The `public-ref:` token:** a PR body line `public-ref: <40-hex sha>` (or the dispatch input) pins the public commit `packcheck` builds packlint from; the default is `main`.<br>• **Retention** is dry-run by default (the newest 10 tagged + the policy pick + their platform manifests); `GITHUB_TOKEN` can list the versions.<br>• **CI hygiene:** evalpack steps run with `shell: bash` (`-eo pipefail`), because the implicit `bash -e` let a failing gate piped into `tee` pass. Gate lines use " — " so setup-go's problem matcher doesn't flag `….go: pass` as an error. | [PR #110](https://github.com/sujaykumarsuman/xlearn/pull/110), xlearn-evalpack#1, `v0.2.0` run 36835276038. Fixed with `COALESCE(…, false)::boolean` (the cast is required or sqlc emits `interface{}`) and pinned by `TestUsageMonthOnAnEmptyAccount` on a real database. **Lesson: an aggregate the fake computes in Go needs a real-database test for its empty case.** (2) one corrupt row was reported once per batch, so a pass logged `skipped=2 pending=1` — a self-contradicting summary read on demand (D34); a pass now remembers what it gave up on, counts each row once and stops when a batch holds nothing new. | 2026-09-29 | **m1-04: M1b identity security floor (merged; ships in `v1.7.0`).**<br>• **CSP (SPA shell):** `default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' https://fonts.googleapis.com; font-src 'self' https://fonts.gstatic.com; img-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; form-action 'self' https://github.com`. `form-action` lists GitHub because Chrome applies it to the redirect after the OAuth start form POST. There is no `'unsafe-inline'` anywhere, and no `style-src` exception was needed. Every response gets `nosniff` + `Referrer-Policy: same-origin`. Mutating `/api` calls need `Sec-Fetch-Site` ∈ {same-origin, none} when the header is present (else 403 `cross_site_request`) and `application/json` (else 415), checked in that order. The OAuth start form POST keeps only the origin check.<br>• **Revoke-all includes the caller:** a password change (or a first set) revokes every session in the same transaction and answers `{ok:true, reauth:true}`. The gateway also clears the cookie, and the SPA lands on `/auth?notice=password_changed`.<br>• **Reads are audited too, and so are refusals:** `list` and `seats` write `admin_audit`. A refused or failed verb rolls back, then writes an audit-only row with `detail.outcome` (`refused_last_owner`, `refused_seat_cap`, `refused_email_taken`, `user_not_found`, `error`). The target is the account id or `-`. No email or user argument is ever recorded.<br>• **Suspended = no session anywhere:** a suspended account gets the uniform 401 on email login after one dummy compare, `/auth?error=account_unavailable` from the OAuth callback, and a 403 `account_unavailable` on dev login.<br>• **The cohort bit is per request, not cached:** `authSession` fills a request-scoped slot that `courseVisible`/`filterCatalog`/`handleStartPath` read. A second `authSession` in the same request reuses it. `handleCreateMistake` now authenticates before the visibility check.<br>• **Route tables:** each service registers its JWT routes from a `userRoutes()` table behind `RequireRole(learner)`, so a new route can't skip the role check (route-walk tests). A token without the role gets 403 `{"error":{"code":"forbidden"}}`.<br>• **Small calls:** an unknown `SIGNUP_MODE` runs closed with an INFO note, and only `open` without `DEV_AUTH` logs ERROR. An invalid `SEAT_CAP` falls back to 15. The CLI tester's `account_created` event says `provider: "email"` (its sign-in method). The admin CLI's PG 18 test joins the CI store-integration step. `/sessions/validate` query cost in compose (pgbench, 5,000 runs): p95 76 µs → 113 µs with the join (account PK; the planner seq-scans a tiny table). No ADR: everything sits inside ADR-0033/0035. | [PR #98](https://github.com/sujaykumarsuman/xlearn/pull/98). Compose smoke: headless Chrome, 28 screen visits (every screen, Settings password change + Connect GitHub, coach SSE, `/u/`), 0 CSP violations, 0 CSP issues. Every CLI verb ran against compose (exit codes 0/3/4, audit rows, guards) |
